atha saptamo'dhyāyaḥ |
.000
atha trayodaśīvratāni |
dharmādapetaṃ na kadācideva yadīyavācī viṣayatvameti |
sa eva hemādriranukrameṇa cayodaśoṣu matavṛndamāha ||
yudhiṣṭhira uvāca |
bhagavan bhūtabhavyeśa saṃsārārthavatāraka |
vrata' kathaya kicinme rūpasaubhāgyadāyakaṃ ||
anaṅgaḥ proṣito yena phalaṃ yacchati keśava 1
bhāvapasobhāgya ta vistarato vada ||
kṛṣṇa uvāca |
anaṅgaḥ śrūyate devaḥ śūlapāṇiḥ pinākabhṛt |
tasmin sampa jise * pārthaṃ kitrāproti narībhuvi ||
A
tena te kathayiṣyāmi śūlapāṇitratevidam |
yava kasyacidākhyātaṃ vratānāmuttamaṃ vrataṃ ||
sorkhā bhaktyā naromatyai yadyadicchati pāṇḍava|
tattadāpnotyasandigdhamanaṅgāsyāṃ vayodaśīm ||
kiṃ vratebahubhiḥ pārtha umāvaphalapradeḥ |
vayodaśau sviyaṃ puṣmā sarvvavrataphalapradā |
kiṃtu pūjita iti pukhakāntare pāṭha: 1
(1)
[[page 23-to-23]]
hemādriḥ | [ vratakhakha 17 pradhyāyaḥ |

catvāri devaśārdūla sarvvāniṣṭaharāṇi tu |
prasaṅgenāpi nudyante tava ye kupitairnarāḥ |
te'pi yānaṃ samāruhya tvāyānti ca śivaṃ padaṃ ||
svargatāḥ kramayogena bhukvātu bahudhā parān |
vicitrarūpa sapacāḥ sarvvakāmasukhapradāḥ ||
kanyā hiraṣṭavarṣāva ponotratapayodharāḥ |
bhuṣyanti suvicitāstāḥ pātaṃ yuñjanti mānavāḥ ||
nārthī vā patanaṃ kuryustadā bhuñjanti bhairavān |
prabhutvaṃ divyabhogādyāḥ śrapsarogaṇasaṃyutāḥ ||
gāndharvvaca yathā yacaṃ kivaraṃ vāruṇaṃ tathā |

svakāmabhogasampadyaṃ patanānūvanaṃ labhet |
kāmatopi tayā cāntaṃ yāti paraṃ padaṃ ||

ratnāni rājagāmīni va bāhanameva ca |
samanyavettasya yadyenaiva raṇe hataṃ ||
kulastriyastu vijñeyāstathā rāma na kasyacit |
svadeśe paradeśe vā sādhvī yatra ca dūṣayet ||
anyathā saṃgarī ghoro bhavatīha jayāvahaḥ |
madhuṃ prāpta raṇe muktaḥ putrastasya prakīrttitaḥ ||
punastena na yoṣavyantasya dharmavidāṃ mataṃ |
deśe deśe ya bhācāra: pāramparyyakramāgataḥ ||
sa eva paripāspaḥ syāt prāpya deyaṃ samocitaḥ |
